Compare corrosion protection by coatings

CL-0065 · VisionariesAI Labs Private Limited · Virtual practical

Aim

Compare matched bare, covered, exposed-strip and ideal-blocking references. Account for separate local iron inventories, finite oxygen/water and retained corrosion product.

Principle and apparatus

Chosen equivalent reaction:4Fe+3O₂+2H₂O→4FeOOH. Real rust is a mixture. Sealed reference cells, equal iron blocks, a prepared upper face with side/back access excluded, inert barrier film, available water through a schematic wick, elapsed-day timer and calculated dry-gas pressure. Relative transmission and bare coefficient are chosen comparison parameters.

Procedure

Write the prediction. Record preparation and film settings. Record all four starting conditions, then run and pause at equal elapsed days. Inspect exposed and covered metal/product separately; note any local iron or shared-water exhaustion. Compare a fully open strip, zero transmission, small damage, dry and no-oxygen references. Record masses, gas space and pressure with units. Apply the segment-wise formulas, check whole-cell mass and write result, conclusion and model limitations.

Prediction

Compare matched days and separate exposed and covered iron. <script>Chosen references only.

Worked calculations

Trial 1: Bare reference

  1. Initial iron amount

    nFe₀ = mFe₀/MFe

    Substitution: 0.15/55.845

    Result: 0.002686005909 mol Fe

  2. Initial available water

    nW₀ = ρW VW₀/MW

    Substitution: 1 × 2.000000/18.015

    Result: 0.1110185956 mol H₂O

  3. Initial metal volume

    VFe₀ = mFe₀/ρFe

    Substitution: 0.15/8

    Result: 0.01875 mL

  4. Inert film volume

    Vfilm = (1−f)Aface hfilm

    Substitution: (1−1.000000) × 0.5 × 0.5 × 0.005

    Result: 0 mL; chosen uniform film

  5. Inert film mass

    mfilm = ρfilm Vfilm

    Substitution: 1.2 × 0

    Result: 0 g; retained unchanged

  6. Initial gas space

    Vgas₀ = Vcell−VFe₀−VW₀−Vfilm

    Substitution: 50.000000−0.01875−2.000000−0

    Result: 47.981250 mL

  7. Initial gas amount

    ngas₀ = P₀(Vgas₀/1000)/(RT)

    Substitution: 101.325 × (47.981250/1000)/(8.314462618 × 298.15)

    Result: 0.001961187749 mol O₂/N₂ reference

  8. Initial oxygen amount

    nO₂₀ = yO₂₀ ngas₀

    Substitution: 0.21 × 0.001961187749

    Result: 4.11849427e-4 mol O₂

  9. Inert gas amount

    nN₂ = (1−yO₂₀)ngas₀

    Substitution: (1−0.21) × 0.001961187749

    Result: 0.001549338322 mol N₂

  10. Initial exposed-zone iron

    nFe,e₀ = f nFe₀

    Substitution: 1.000000 × 0.002686005909

    Result: 0.002686005909 mol Fe

  11. Initial covered-zone iron

    nFe,c₀ = (1−f)nFe₀

    Substitution: (1−1.000000) × 0.002686005909

    Result: 0 mol Fe

  12. Chosen exposed-zone weight

    we = f

    Substitution: 1.000000

    Result: 1.000000 dimensionless; zero after local iron exhaustion

  13. Chosen covered-zone weight

    wc = (1−f)p

    Substitution: (1−1.000000) × 0.02

    Result: 0 dimensionless; relative access, not measured permeability

  14. Exposed-zone reacted iron

    ξzone = ΣΔO₂,zone/0.75

    Substitution: 0/0.75

    Result: 0 mol Fe

  15. Exposed-zone iron remaining

    mFe,zone = (nFe,zone₀−ξzone)MFe

    Substitution: (0.002686005909−0) × 55.845

    Result: 0.15 g Fe; finite local inventory

  16. Exposed-zone product

    mproduct,zone = ξzone MFeOOH

    Substitution: 0 × 88.851

    Result: 0 g FeOOH equivalent

  17. Covered-zone reacted iron

    ξzone = ΣΔO₂,zone/0.75

    Substitution: 0/0.75

    Result: 0 mol Fe

  18. Covered-zone iron remaining

    mFe,zone = (nFe,zone₀−ξzone)MFe

    Substitution: (0−0) × 55.845

    Result: 0 g Fe; finite local inventory

  19. Covered-zone product

    mproduct,zone = ξzone MFeOOH

    Substitution: 0 × 88.851

    Result: 0 g FeOOH equivalent

  20. Total reacted iron

    ξ = ξexposed+ξcovered

    Substitution: 0+0

    Result: 0 mol Fe

  21. Total retained product

    mproduct = mproduct,e+mproduct,c

    Substitution: 0+0

    Result: 0 g; all product retained attached

  22. Oxygen remaining

    nO₂ = nO₂₀−ΣΔO₂

    Substitution: 4.11849427e-4−0

    Result: 4.11849427e-4 mol O₂; stable interval exponential

  23. Water remaining

    mW = (nW₀−(2/3)ΣΔO₂)MW

    Substitution: (0.1110185956−(2/3) × 0) × 18.015

    Result: 2.000000 g; zero at shared-water exhaustion

  24. Current metal volume

    VFe = mFe/ρFe

    Substitution: 0.15/8

    Result: 0.01875 mL

  25. Current product volume

    Vproduct = mproduct/ρproduct

    Substitution: 0/4.2

    Result: 0 mL; chosen drawing density

  26. Current gas space

    Vgas = Vcell−VFe−Vproduct−VW−Vfilm

    Substitution: 50.000000−0.01875−0−2.000000−0

    Result: 47.981250 mL

  27. Current dry-gas pressure

    P = (nO₂+nN₂)RT/(Vgas/1000)

    Substitution: (4.11849427e-4+0.001549338322) × 8.314462618 × 298.15/(47.981250/1000)

    Result: 101.325000 kPa; no vapour/dissolved oxygen storage

  28. Retained specimen including film

    mspecimen = mFe+mproduct+mfilm

    Substitution: 0.15+0+0

    Result: 0.15 g

  29. Whole-cell material mass

    mtotal = mspecimen+mW+mO₂+mN₂

    Substitution: 0.15+2.000000+0.01317835797+0.04340316374

    Result: 2.206582 g; cell/wick/mask hardware excluded consistently

  30. Initial whole-cell material mass

    mtotal₀ = mFe₀+mW₀+mO₂₀+mN₂+mfilm

    Substitution: 0.15+2.000000+0.01317835797+0.04340316374+0

    Result: 2.206582 g; equals current whole-cell material mass

  31. Current chosen oxygen consumption rate

    rO₂ = kbare (we,active+wc,active)nO₂

    Substitution: 0.4 × (1.000000+0) × 4.11849427e-4

    Result: 1.64739771e-4 mol O₂/day; not measured corrosion-rate calibration

Result, conclusion and limitations

Generic access settings affect the reference rate; finite local iron and shared water can stop a channel. <img src=x> No real-paint ranking.

Generic barrier comparison, not a measured paint/oil/wax ranking, protection lifetime, galvanic mechanism or physical corrosion prediction. Fixed25°C idealO₂/N₂ gas, chosen parallel-zone kinetics, finite local iron/shared oxygen/water, retained FeOOH-equivalent product and uniform volume-based layers. Only the prepared upper face reacts. Film follows the covered reference top; no mechanical cracking/lifting/adhesion, pitting, under-film lateral transport, evolving coating, dissolved oxygen/vapour storage, sorption, wetting, heat or salt chemistry solver. Drawing densitiesFe8/product4.2/water1/film1.2g/mL and film thickness0.005cm are chosen references. Ideal blocking does not establish that real coatings are permanently impermeable. Quantitative formulas are teacher extensions; class placement editorial.
